        <body>

            <div ana="Sūtrasthāna" n="1" type="sthāna">

                <head xml:lang="en">[Sūtrasthāna 1-13]</head>

                <div n="1" type="adhyāya">

                    <milestone facs="dscn2977 fol 001.jpg upper" n="1v" unit="folio"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.0"><l><pb facs="3" n="1v"/>oṃ namo dhanvantaraye ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.1"><l>athāto vedotpattiṃ nāmādhyāyaṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.3"><l>atha khalu bhagavantam amaravaram
                        ṛṣigaṇaparivṛtam āśramasthaṃ kāśirājaṃ divodāsaṃ dhanvantarim
                            aupadhe<lb n="2"/>nava<pc> </pc>vairaṇorabhra<pc> </pc>puṣkalāvata<pc> </pc>karavīra<pc> </pc>gopurarakṣita<pc> </pc>bhoja<pc> </pc>suśruta<pc> </pc>prabhṛtaya ūcur </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.4"><l>bhagavac chārīramānasāgantubhir vvyādhibhir
                        vvividhavedanābhighātopadrutāṃ sanāthān anā<lb n="3"/>thavad
                        viceṣṭamānān vikrośataś ca mānavānām abhisamīkṣya manasi naḥ
                        pīḍābhavat | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.4b"><l> teṣāṃ sukhaiṣiṇāṃ rogopaśamārtham ātmanaś ca
                        prāṇayātrārtham āyurvvedam icchāma<lb n="4"/> upadiśyamānam
                        attrāya uttam auhikam āmuṣmikañ ca śreyas tad bhagavantam upasannā
                        smaḥ śiṣyatveneti ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.5"><l>tān uvāca bhagavān svāgatam vaḥ sarvva
                            evāmīmāṃsyā<lb n="5"/> adhyāpyāś ca bhagavanto vatsāḥ |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.6"><l> iha khalv āyurvvedo nāma yad upāṅgam
                        atharvvavedasyoktam anutpādyaiva ca prajāḥ ślokaśatasahasram
                        adhyāyasahasrañ ca kṛtavān svayambhū<lb n="7"/>r
                        alpāyuṣkālpamedhastvañ ca alokya narāṇām bhūyo 'ṣṭadhā praṇītavān
                        | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.7"><l> tad yathā śalyaṃ
                        śālākyaṅ<!--not 100% certain about the ṅka, maybe 75%-->
                        kāyacikitsā bhūtavidyā kaumārabhṛtyam agadatantra rasāyanatantraṃ
                            <pb ana="dscn2977                             fol001.jpg lower" facs="3" n="2v"/><lb n="1"/>vājīkaraṇatantram iti |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.1"><l>athāsya pratyekāṅgalakṣaṇasamāsas</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.1a"><l> tatra śalyan nāma
                            vividhatṛṇakāṣṭhapāṣāṇapāṃśuloṣṭāsthibālanakhapūyāśrāvaduṣṭavraṇāntargarbhaśalyo<lb n="1"/>ddharaṇārthaṃ
                        yantraśastrakṣārāgnipraṇidhānaviniścayārthañ ca ṣaṣṭyābhidhānair
                        iti |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.2"><l>śālākyatantran nāmordhvajātrugatānāṃ vikārāṇāṃ
                        śravaṇanayana <note place="top-margin">vadana</note>
                            vadanaghrāṇādisaṃśritānāṃ<lb n="2"/> vikārāṇām
                        upaśamakaraṇārthaṃ |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.3"><l>kāyacikītsā nāma sarvvaśarīrāvasthitānāṃ
                        vyādhīnām upaśamakaraṇārthaṃ
                            jvaraśophagulmaraktapittonmādāpasmārapramehātīsārā<lb n="3"/>dīnāñ ca |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.4"><l>bhūtavidyā nāma
                        devadānavagandharvvayakṣarākṣasapitṛpiśācavināyakanāgagrahopasṛṣṭacetasāṃ
                        śāntikarmabaliharaṇādigrahopaśamanārthaṃ |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.5"><l>kaumārabhṛ<lb n="4"/>tyan nāma
                        kumārābharaṇadhātrīkṣīradoṣasaṃśodhanārthaṃ
                        duṣṭastanyagrahasamutthitānāñ ca vyādhīnām upaśamakaraṇārthaṃ
                        |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.6"><l> agadatantran nāma sarppakīṭalūtādaṣṭasarīsṛ<lb n="5"/>paviṣavyañjanārtham vividhaviṣavegopaśamanārthañ ca
                        ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.7"><l> rasāyanatantran nāma vayaḥsthāpanam
                        āyurmedhākaraṇaṃ vyādhyupaśamakaraṇārthañ ca |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.8.8"><l> vājīkaraṇatantran nāma svalpaduṣṭakṣī<pb ana="dscn2978 fol 002.jpg upper" facs="4" n="2v"/><lb n="1"/>ṇaviśuṣkaretasāṃ śukrāpy āyatanaprasādopacayajanananimittaṃ
                        praharṣajananārthañ ca |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.9"><l> evam ayam āyurvvedo ṣṭāṅga upadiśyate atra
                        kasmai kim varṇyatām iti </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.10"><l>ta ūcur asmākaṃ sa<lb n="2"/>rvvam eva
                        śalyajñānam alaṃkṛtvopadiśatu bhagavān iti </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.11"><l> sa uvācaivam astv iti</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.12"><l>ta ūcur bhūyo 'smākaṃ sarvveṣām
                            evaikamatīnā<unclear>ṃ</unclear> matam abhisamīkṣya suśruto
                        bhagavantam pratyakṣa<lb n="3"/><g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/>syopadiśyamānaṃ vayam apy upadhārayiṣyāmaḥ |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.13"><l>sa uvācaivam astv iti | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.14-15"><l> iha khalv āyurvvede prayojanaṃ
                        vyādhyupasṛṣṭasya vyādhiparimokṣaḥ svastharakṣaṇañ cāyur asmin
                            vida<lb n="4"/>nty anena cāyu<add place="top">rvvidyata ity
                            āyu</add>rvvedas</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.16"><l>tasyāṅgavaram āgamapratyakṣānumānopamānair
                        aviruddham ucyamānam upadhārayadhvam </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.17"><l> etad dhy aṅgam prathamam pradhānam prāg
                        abhihitatvād vraṇasaṃrohaṇakaratvā<lb n="5"/>d
                        yajñaśiraḥpradhānasandhānāc ca śrūyate hi yathā purā rudreṇa <subst>
                            <add place="bottom-margin">yajña</add>
                        </subst>śiraś chinnam aśvibhyām punaḥ sandhitam ity </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.18"><l> aṣṭānām api cāyurvvedatantrāṇām etad evādhikam
                        āśu kriyā<lb n="6"/>karaṇād yantraśastrakṣārāgnipraṇidhānāt
                        sarvvatantrasāmānyāc ca |</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.19"><l> tad idaṃ śāśvatam puṇyaṃ svargyāṃ yaṃ <subst>
                            <add place="bottom-margin">ya</add>
                        </subst>śasyam āyuṣyaṃ vṛttikarañ ca | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.20"><l> tad brahmovāca tat prajāpatir adhijage <pb facs="4" n="3r"/><lb n="1"/>tasmād aśvināv aśvibhyām indra
                        indrād aḥaṃ mayā tv iha pradeyam arthibhyaḥ prajāhitahetoḥ ||</l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.21"><l>
                        <expan>bha<ex>vati cātra</ex></expan> ||
                        <caesura/>
                         ahaṃ hi dhanvantarir ādidevo jarārujāmṛtyuharo 'marāṇāṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         śalyam mahac chā<lb n="2"/>stravaraṃ gṛhītvā prāpto 'smi gam
                        bhūya ihopadeṣṭuṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.1.22"><l> tatrāsmiñ śā<!--Not sure about the ñśā-->stre
                        pañcamahābhūtaśarīrisamavāyaḥ puruṣa ity ucyate | tasmin kriyā so
                        'dhiṣṭhānaṃ kasmāl lokadvaividhyā<lb n="3"/>l loko hi dvividhan
                        sthāvaro jaṅgamaś ca dvividhātmaka evāgneyaḥ saumyaś ca
                        tadbhūyastvāt pañcātmako vā | tatra caturvvidho bhūtagrāmaḥ
                        saṃsvedajo hridajarāyujāṇḍajasaṃjñās tasmi<lb n="4"/>n puruṣaḥ
                        pradhānas tasyopakaraṇam anyat tasmāt puruṣo 'dhiṣṭhānaṃ </l></lg>

                    <milestone n="33v4" unit="jpeg"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.1"><l> athāta āma<lb n="5"/>pakveṣaṇīyam vyākhyāsyāmaḥ
                        || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.3"><l> atha śophasamutthānā gra<gap n="1" unit="char"/><g ref="#newa-double-comma" type="punctuation"/>nthividradhyalajīprabhṛtayaḥ | prāyeṇa vyādhayo 'bhihitā
                        anekākṛtayaḥ | tair vvilakṣaṇaḥ pṛthur ggra<g ref="#newa-double-comma" type="punctuation"/><lb n="6"/><gap n="11" unit="char"/>thitaḥ samo viṣamo vā
                            tvad<unclear>māṃ</unclear>sasthāyī saṃghātaḥ
                            śarī<unclear>rai</unclear>kadeśotthitaḥ śopha ity ucyate | </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.17.4"><l> sa ṣaḍvidho bhavati | vātapittakapha<add place="below">śoṇita</add>sannipātāgantu<g ref="#newa-double-comma" type="punctuation"/><pb ana="dscn3009 fol 033.jpg lower" facs="35" n="34r"/><lb n="1"/>kanimittaḥ | tatra vātaśvayathur aruṇaḥ kṛṣṇo vā paruṣo

                    <milestone facs="dscn3023 fol 047.jpg upper" n="47v1" unit="jpeg"/>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.23.1"><l> athātaḥ kṛtyākṛtyavidhiṃ vyākhyāsyāmaḥ || </l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.23.3"><l> tatra vayaḥsthānān dṛḍhānām prāṇavatāṃ
                        satvavatām ātmavatāñ ca sucikitsyā vraṇā bhavanti || <lb n="2"/>ekaikasmin vā puruṣe yatraitad guṇapañcakam bhavati | tasya
                        khalu sā<cb/>dhanīyatamāḥ | tatra vayaḥsthānām pratyagradhātutvād
                        āśu vraṇasaṃroho bhavati | dṛḍhānāṃ sthirabahumāṃsatvā<lb n="3"/>c
                        chastram avacāryamāṇaṃ sirāsnāyvādīn viśeṣān na prāpnoti
                        prāṇa<cb/>vatām vedanābhighātāhārayantraṇādibhir nna glānir
                        bbhavati | satvavatān dāruṇair api kriyāviśeṣair nna vyathā bha<lb n="4"/>vati | ātmavatām punar mmitāhāravihārādibhir upadeśair
                            nnānya<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><cb/>thā matiḥ pravarttate |
                        sarvvañ caiṣāṃ sādhur bbhavati | tasmād eṣāṃ sukhasādhanīyatamāḥ
                        || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.23.4"><l> ta eva viparītaguṇāḥ | <lb n="5"/>vṛddhakṛṣālpaprāṇabhīruṣv anātmavatsu ca draṣṭavyāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.23.5"><l> sphikpāyu<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/><cb/>prajananalalāṭagaṇḍauṣṭhaphalakoṣodarajatrumukhābhyantarasaṃsthāḥ
                        sukharopaṇīyā vraṇāḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.23.6"><l> akṣidanta<lb n="6"/>nāsāapāṅgaśrotranābhijaṭharasevanīnitambapārśvakukṣistanakakṣasandhibhāgagatāḥ
                        saphenapūyānilaraktavāhinontaḥśalyāś ca duścikitsyāḥ ||

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.17"><l> khebhyaḥ saromakūpebhyo yasya raktaṃ
                        pravarttate |
                        <caesura/>
                         puruṣasyāviṣārttasya sa pa <lb n="2"/>rāsur asaṃśayaṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.18"><l> vātāṣṭhīlā tu hṛdaye yasyorddham anuyāyinī |
                        <caesura/>
                        <cb/>rujānnavidveṣakarī sa parāsur asaṃśayaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.19"><l> ananyopadravakṛtaḥ pādaḥ śophasamutthitaḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         puruṣaṃ<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/>
                        <lb n="3"/>hanti nārīñ ca mukhajo guhyajo dvayaṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.20"><l> atīsāro jvaro dhmānañ cha<cb/>rddiḥ
                        sūnāṅgameḍhratā |
                        <caesura/>
                         kāsi<unclear reason="faded">na</unclear>ḥ śvāsino vāpi yasya taṃ
                        kṣīṇam ādiśet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.21"><l> svedo dāhaś ca balavān hikkā <lb n="4"/>śvāsaś
                        ca mānavaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         balavantam api prāṇair vviyuñjanti na saṃśayaṃ <cb/>|| </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.22"><l> śyāvā jihvā bhaved yasya savyañ cākṣi
                        nimajjati |
                        <caesura/>
                         mukhañ ca jāyate pūtir yasya tam parivarjjayet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.23"><l> netre <lb n="5"/>cāmreṇa pūryete svidyete
                        caraṇau tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         cakṣuś cākulatāṃ yāti <cb/>yamarāṣṭraṅ gamiṣyataḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.24"><l> atimātraṃ laghūni syur ggātrāṇy atigurūṇi vā |
                        <caesura/>
                         yasyākasmāt sa vijñeyo gantā <lb n="6"/>caiva yamālayaṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.25"><l> paṅkamatsyavasātaila<del rend="erasing">ṃ</del>ghṛtagandhām̐ś ca ye narāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         viṣṭagandhām̐ś ca ye vānti gatās te yamasādanaṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.26"><l> yūkā lalāṭam āyānti balin nāśnanti vāyasāḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         yeṣāṃ <pb ana="dscn3040 fol 064.jpg lower" facs="66" n="65r"/>
                        <lb n="1"/>cāpi ratir nnāsti gatās te yamasādanaṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.27"><l> jvarātīsāraśophāḥ syur yasyānyonyāvasādinaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         prakṣīṇañ ca balaṃ yasya na sa śakyaś cikitsituṃ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.28"><l> kṣīṇasya yasya kṣuttṛṣṇe hṛdyai<lb n="2"/>r
                        mmṛṣṭair hitais tathā |
                        <caesura/>
                         annapānair nna śāsyeta tasya mṛtyur upasthitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.29"><l>
                        <cb/>pravāhikā śiraḥśūlaṃ koṣṭhaśūlañ ca dāruṇaṃ |
                        <caesura/>
                         pipāsā balahāniś ca tasya mṛtyur upasthitaḥ || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.30"><l>
                        <lb n="3"/>viṣameṇopacāreṇa karmmabhiś ca purākṛtaiḥ |
                        <caesura/>
                         anityatvāc ca jantū<cb/>nāṃ jīvitan nidhanaṃ vrajet || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.31"><l> pretā dūtāḥ piśācāś ca rakṣāṃsi vividhāni ca |
                        <caesura/>
                         maraṇīyan naran nitya<g ref="#newa-gap-filler"/>m upasarppanti
                        sarvvadā || </l></lg>

                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.32"><l> tāni bhaiṣajavīryāṇi pratinighnanti sarvvadā
                        <cb/>|
                        <caesura/>
                         tasmāt mohāḥ kriyāḥ sarvvā bhavantīha gatāyuṣam</l></lg>


                    <lg xml:id="SS.1.31.trailer"><l> iti || 31 || </l></lg>
